习题 4.72 考虑电荷量为 $q$ 质量为 $m$ 和自旋为 $s$ 的粒子处于均匀磁场 $B _ { 0 }$ 中.磁矢势可以选择为

$$
\boldsymbol {A} = - \frac {1}{2} \boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}.
$$

(a) 验证该矢势产生一个均匀的磁场 $B _ { 0 }$   
(b) 证明:哈密顿量可以写为

$$
H = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + q \varphi - \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \cdot (\gamma_ {0} \boldsymbol {L} + \gamma \boldsymbol {S}) + \frac {q ^ {2}}{8 m} \left[ r ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2} - (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) ^ {2} \right],
$$

其中 $\gamma _ { 0 } = q / 2 m$ 是回转旋磁比.注释 $\scriptstyle { B _ { 0 } }$ 。中的线性项使得磁矩(轨道和自旋)在能量上有利于沿磁场方向排列 这就是 颐磁性的起源 $\scriptstyle { B _ { 0 } }$ 的二次项会导致相反的效应 抗磁性

解答 (a)

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {B} = \nabla \times \boldsymbol {A} = - \frac {1}{2} \nabla \times (\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) \\ = - \frac {1}{2} (- 2 \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) \\ = B _ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$\nabla \times ( \boldsymbol { r } \times \boldsymbol { B } _ { 0 } )$ 的详细推导过程如下.

首先

$$
\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} \hat {x} & \hat {y} & \hat {z} \\ x & y & z \\ B _ {0 x} & B _ {0 y} & B _ {0 z} \end{array} \right) = (y B _ {0 z} - z B _ {0 y}) \hat {x} + (z B _ {0 x} - x B _ {0 z}) \hat {y} + (x B _ {0 y} - y B _ {0 x}) \hat {z},
$$

因此

$$
\begin{array}{l} \nabla \times (\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) = \left(\frac {\partial}{\partial x} \hat {x} + \frac {\partial}{\partial y} \hat {y} + \frac {\partial}{\partial z} \hat {z}\right) \\ \times \left[ \left(y B _ {0 z} - z B _ {0 y}\right) \hat {x} + \left(z B _ {0 x} - x B _ {0 z}\right) \hat {y} + \left(x B _ {0 y} - y B _ {0 x}\right) \hat {z} \right] \\ = \left( \begin{array}{c c c} \hat {x} & \hat {y} & \hat {z} \\ \frac {\partial}{\partial x} & \frac {\partial}{\partial y} & \frac {\partial}{\partial z} \\ y B _ {0 z} - z B _ {0 y} & z B _ {0 x} - x B _ {0 z} & x B _ {0 y} - y B _ {0 x} \end{array} \right) \\ = \hat {x} \left[ \frac {\partial (x B _ {0 y} - y B _ {0 x})}{\partial y} - \frac {\partial (z B _ {0 x} - x B _ {0 z})}{\partial z} \right] \\ + \hat {y} \left[ \frac {\partial (y B _ {0 z} - z B _ {0 y})}{\partial z} - \frac {\partial (x B _ {0 y} - y B _ {0 x})}{\partial x} \right] \\ + \hat {z} \left[ \frac {\partial (z B _ {0 x} - x B _ {0 z})}{\partial x} - \frac {\partial (y B _ {0 z} - z B _ {0 y})}{\partial y} \right] \\ = \hat {x} (- 2 B _ {0 x}) + \hat {y} (- 2 B _ {0 y}) + \hat {z} (- 2 B _ {0 z}) \\ = - 2 B _ {0 x} \hat {x} - 2 B _ {0 y} \hat {y} - 2 B _ {0 z} \hat {z} \\ = - 2 B _ {0}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) 磁场中的带电粒子，其哈密顿量可以结合方程 (4.158) (4.188) 写为

$$
H = \frac {1}{2 m} \left(\boldsymbol {p} - q \boldsymbol {A}\right) ^ {2} + q \varphi - \gamma \boldsymbol {S} \cdot \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - \frac {q}{2 m} \left(\boldsymbol {p} \cdot \boldsymbol {A} + \boldsymbol {A} \cdot \boldsymbol {p}\right) + \frac {q}{2 m} A ^ {2} + q \varphi - \gamma \boldsymbol {S} \cdot \boldsymbol {B} _ {0},
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} A ^ {2} = \frac {1}{4} (\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) \cdot (\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) = \frac {1}{4} \left\{\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \left[ \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \times (\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) \right] \right\} \\ = \frac {1}{4} \boldsymbol {r} \cdot \left[ \boldsymbol {r} \cdot B _ {0} ^ {2} - \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \left(\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}\right) \right] \\ = \frac {1}{4} \left[ r ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2} - \left(\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}\right) ^ {2} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

将 $p \cdot A + A \cdot p$ 作用到一个测试函数 $f$ 1-..，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {p} \cdot (\boldsymbol {A} f) + \boldsymbol {A} \cdot (\boldsymbol {p} f) = - \mathrm {i} \hbar \nabla \cdot (\boldsymbol {A} f) - \mathrm {i} \hbar \boldsymbol {A} \cdot (\nabla f) \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar (\nabla \cdot \boldsymbol {A}) f - \mathrm {i} \hbar \boldsymbol {A} \cdot (\nabla f) - \mathrm {i} \hbar \boldsymbol {A} \cdot (\nabla f) \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar (\nabla \cdot \boldsymbol {A}) f - \mathrm {i} \hbar 2 \boldsymbol {A} \cdot (\nabla f) \\ = - \mathrm {i} \hbar \left[ - \frac {1}{2} \nabla \cdot (\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) f - \frac {1}{2} 2 (\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) \cdot (\nabla f) \right] \\ = \frac {1}{2} \mathrm {i} \hbar [ \nabla \cdot (\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) f + 2 (\boldsymbol {r} \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) \cdot (\nabla f) ] \\ = \frac {1}{2} \mathrm {i} \hbar [ \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \cdot (\nabla \times \boldsymbol {r}) f - \boldsymbol {r} \cdot (\nabla \times \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) f - 2 \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \cdot (\boldsymbol {r} \times \nabla f) ] \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = - \mathrm {i} \hbar \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \cdot (\boldsymbol {r} \times \nabla f) \\ = \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \cdot \boldsymbol {L} f. \\ \end{array}
$$

将以上几式代入哈密顿量，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} H = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - \frac {q}{2 m} \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \cdot \boldsymbol {L} + \frac {q}{2 m} \frac {1}{4} \left[ r ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2} - (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) ^ {2} \right] + q \varphi - \gamma \boldsymbol {S} \cdot \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \\ = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + q \varphi - \boldsymbol {B} _ {0} \cdot (\gamma_ {0} \boldsymbol {L} + \gamma \boldsymbol {S}) + \frac {q ^ {2}}{8 m} \left[ r ^ {2} B _ {0} ^ {2} - (\boldsymbol {r} \cdot \boldsymbol {B} _ {0}) ^ {2} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

习题 4.73 例题 .4以力的形式表述施特恩格拉赫效应的准经典解释.从自旋为1/2 的中性粒子穿过磁场的哈密顿量开始，磁场由方程 (4.169) 给出，

$$
\boldsymbol {H} = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - \gamma \boldsymbol {B} \cdot \boldsymbol {S},
$$

用广义的埃伦菲斯特定理(方程 (3.73)) 证明:

$$
m \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} t ^ {2}} \left\langle z \right\rangle = \gamma \alpha \left\langle S _ {z} \right\rangle .
$$

注释 方程(4.170) 是一个正确的量子力学陈述，这里是把量子数理解为期望值.

证明 由方程 (3.73)

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} \left\langle \hat {Q} \right\rangle = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \left[ \hat {H}, \hat {Q} \right] \right\rangle + \left\langle \frac {\partial \hat {Q}}{\partial t} \right\rangle ,
$$

得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} \langle z \rangle = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \langle [ H, z ] \rangle = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \left[ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - \gamma \boldsymbol {B} \cdot \boldsymbol {S}, z \right] \right\rangle = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \left[ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m}, z \right] \right\rangle \\ = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{2 m \hbar} \left\langle \left[ p ^ {2}, z \right] \right\rangle = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{2 m \hbar} \left\langle p _ {z} \left[ p _ {z}, z \right] + \left[ p _ {z}, z \right] p _ {z} \right\rangle \\ = \frac {\mathrm {i}}{2 m \hbar} \left\langle - 2 \mathrm {i} \hbar p _ {z} \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{m} \left\langle p _ {z} \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

再次求导，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} t ^ {2}} \langle z \rangle = \frac {1}{m} \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} t} \left\langle p _ {z} \right\rangle = \frac {1}{m} \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle [ H, p _ {z} ] \right\rangle = \frac {1}{m} \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \left[ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - \gamma \boldsymbol {B} \cdot \boldsymbol {S}, p _ {z} \right] \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{m} \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \langle [ - \gamma \boldsymbol {B} \cdot \boldsymbol {S}, p _ {z} ] \rangle \\ = - \gamma \frac {1}{m} \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \langle [ B, p _ {z} ] \rangle \cdot S. \\ \end{array}
$$

由方程 (4.169) $\boldsymbol { B } = - \alpha x \boldsymbol { \hat { i } } + \left( B _ { 0 } + \alpha z \right) \boldsymbol { \hat { k } } .$ ，得

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm {d} t ^ {2}} \left\langle z \right\rangle = - \gamma \frac {1}{m} \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \left\langle \left[ - \alpha x \hat {i} + (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \hat {k}, p _ {z} \right] \right\rangle \cdot \boldsymbol {S} = - \gamma \frac {1}{m} \frac {\mathrm {i}}{\hbar} \alpha \left\langle [ z, p _ {z} ] \right\rangle \hat {k} \cdot \boldsymbol {S} = \frac {\gamma}{m} \alpha S _ {z}.
$$

习题 4.74 实际上，无论是例题 .4还是习题 4.73 都没有真正解决施特恩一格拉赫实验的薛定海方程.在本题中，我们将看到如何建立这个计算.自旋为 1/2 的中性粒子穿过施特恩一格拉赫实验装置的哈密顿量为

$$
\boldsymbol {H} = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - \gamma \boldsymbol {B} \cdot \boldsymbol {S},
$$

其中 $\textbf {  { B } }$ 由方程( 给出.对于自旋为 1/2 的粒子，包含空间和自旋两部分自由度的波函数最一般形式是① ①

$$
\boldsymbol {\Psi} (\boldsymbol {r}, t) = \Psi_ {+} (\boldsymbol {r}, t) \chi_ {+} + \Psi_ {-} (\boldsymbol {r}, t) \chi_ {-}.
$$

(a) 将 $\pmb { { \cal { V } } } \left( \pmb { r } , t \right)$ 代入薛定谤方程

$$
H \boldsymbol {\Psi} = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \boldsymbol {\Psi}
$$

获得一对 $\varPsi _ { \pm }$ 的精合方程.部分答案:

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \nabla^ {2} \varPsi_ {+} - \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \varPsi_ {+} + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \varPsi_ {-} = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \varPsi_ {+}.
$$

(b) 从例题 4.3 知道自旋在均匀磁场 $B _ { 0 } \hat { k }$ 中做进动.可以将这部分从解中提取出来作为系数因子，不失一般性地可以写为

$$
\varPsi_ {\pm} \left(\boldsymbol {r}, t\right) = \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \gamma B _ {0} t / 2} \tilde {\varPsi} \left(\boldsymbol {r}, t\right).
$$

求精合方程岳 $\tilde { \psi } _ { \pm }$ ·部分答案:

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \nabla^ {2} \tilde {\Psi} _ {+} - \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha z \tilde {\Psi} _ {+} + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} \gamma B _ {0} t} \tilde {\Psi} _ {-} = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \tilde {\Psi} _ {+}.
$$

(c) 如果忽略 (b) 中的振动项一一由于其平均值为军(见例题 4.4 的讨论)可以获得非精合方程的形式

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \nabla^ {2} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm} + V _ {\pm} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm} = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm}.
$$

基于以上粒子在"势" $V _ { \pm }$ 的运动，解释施特恩一格拉赫实验.

解答 (8) 哈密顿量为

$$
H = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - \gamma \boldsymbol {B} \cdot \boldsymbol {S} = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - \gamma \left(B _ {x} S _ {x} + B _ {y} S _ {y} + B _ {z} S _ {z}\right),
$$

将自旋 $S _ { x }$ 和 $S _ { y }$ 用自旋降阶算符表示，方程 (4.16θ) 磁场 $\boldsymbol { B } = - \alpha x \boldsymbol { \hat { i } } + \left( B _ { 0 } + \alpha z \right) \boldsymbol { \hat { k } }$ ，代入上式，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} H = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} - \gamma \left(B _ {x} \frac {S _ {+} + S _ {-}}{2} + B _ {y} \frac {S _ {+} - S _ {-}}{2} + B _ {z} S _ {z}\right) \\ = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} + \frac {1}{2} \gamma \alpha x (S _ {+} + S _ {-}) - \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) S _ {z}. \\ \end{array}
$$

上式哈密顿量作用在波函数 $\psi \left( r , t \right) = \psi _ { + } \left( r , t \right) \chi _ { + } + \psi _ { - } \left( r , t \right) \chi _ { - }$ 上，得

$$
H \Psi = H [ \Psi_ {+} (r, t) \chi_ {+} + \Psi_ {-} (r, t) \chi_ {-} ]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \Psi_ {+} (r, t) \chi_ {+} + \frac {1}{2} \gamma \alpha x (S _ {+} + S _ {-}) \Psi_ {+} (r, t) \chi_ {+} - \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) S _ {z} \Psi_ {+} (r, t) \chi_ {+} \\ + \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \Psi_ {-} (r, t) \chi_ {-} + \frac {1}{2} \gamma \alpha x (S _ {+} + S _ {-}) \Psi_ {-} (r, t) \chi_ {-} - \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) S _ {z} \Psi_ {-} (r, t) \chi_ {-} \\ = \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \Psi_ {+} (r, t) \chi_ {+} + \frac {1}{2} \gamma \alpha x \Psi_ {+} (r, t) \hbar \chi_ {-} - \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \Psi_ {+} (r, t) \frac {\hbar}{2} \chi_ {+} \\ + \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \Psi_ {-} (r, t) \chi_ {-} + \frac {1}{2} \gamma \alpha x \Psi_ {-} (r, t) \hbar \chi_ {+} - \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \Psi_ {-} (r, t) \left(- \frac {\hbar}{2}\right) \chi_ {-} \\ = \left[ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \Psi_ {+} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \Psi_ {-} (r, t) - \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \Psi_ {+} (r, t) \right] \chi_ {+} \\ + \left[ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \Psi_ {-} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \Psi_ {+} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \Psi_ {-} (r, t) \right] \chi_ {-}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由 $\mathrm { i } \hbar \frac { \partial } { \partial t } \varPsi = \mathrm { i } \hbar \frac { \partial \varPsi _ { + } } { \partial t } \chi _ { + } + \mathrm { i } \hbar \frac { \partial \varPsi _ { - } } { \partial t } \chi _ { - } = H \varPsi$ 4 θ-  χ+ /\. T +iñ , $\chi \pm$ 是相互正变的，因此对应的薛定诗方程的系数一定是相等的，即

$$
\frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \Psi_ {+} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \Psi_ {-} (r, t) - \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \Psi_ {+} (r, t) = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \Psi_ {+}}{\partial t},
$$

$$
\frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \Psi_ {-} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \Psi_ {+} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \Psi_ {-} (r, t) = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \Psi_ {-}}{\partial t}.
$$

或

$$
\frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \varPsi_ {\pm} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \varPsi_ {\mp} (r, t) \mp \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \varPsi_ {\pm} (r, t) = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \varPsi_ {\pm}}{\partial t}.
$$

(b) 由! $\Psi _ { \pm } \left( r , t \right) = \mathrm { e } ^ { \pm \mathrm { i } \gamma B _ { 0 } t / 2 } \tilde { \Psi } _ { \pm } \left( r , t \right)$ ，得

$$
\frac {\partial \varPsi_ {\pm}}{\partial t} = \pm \frac {\mathrm {i}}{2} \gamma B _ {0} \tilde {\varPsi} _ {\pm} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \gamma B _ {0} t / 2} \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \tilde {\varPsi} _ {\pm},
$$

将其代入 (a) 中的结论，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \varPsi_ {\pm} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \varPsi_ {\mp} (r, t) \mp \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \varPsi_ {\pm} (r, t) = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \varPsi_ {\pm}}{\partial t}. \\ \frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \gamma B _ {0} t / 2} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \mathrm {e} ^ {\mp \mathrm {i} \gamma B _ {0} t / 2} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\mp} (r, t) \mp \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma (B _ {0} + \alpha z) \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \gamma B _ {0} t / 2} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm} (r, t) \\ = \mathrm {i} \hbar \left(\pm \frac {\mathrm {i}}{2} \gamma B _ {0} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm} + \mathrm {e} ^ {\pm \mathrm {i} \gamma B _ {0} t / 2} \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

进一步化筒，得

$$
\frac {p ^ {2}}{2 m} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm} (r, t) + \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha x \mathrm {e} ^ {\mp \mathrm {i} \gamma B _ {0} t} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\mp} (r, t) \mp \frac {\hbar}{2} \gamma \alpha z \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm} (r, t) = \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial}{\partial t} \tilde {\Psi} _ {\pm}.
$$

(c) 忽略 (b) 中结论的振动项，我们很明显地可以看出势场为 $V _ { \pm } = \mp \gamma \alpha \hbar z / 2$ 是一个结性势场.对于自旋向上的态，沿着 $z$ 轴正方向，势杨在减小.对于自旋向下的态，捆着 $z$ 轴负方向，势场在幢小.因此，粒子的初始态有着 $\varPsi _ { + } = \varPsi _ { - }$ ，一且粒子进入磁场， $\varPsi _ { + }$ 十沿着 $z$ 轴正方向传播， $\varPsi _ { - }$ V沿着 $z$ 轴负方向传播.

习题 4.75 考虑例题 4.6 中的系统，穿过螺线管随时间变化的通量为 $\varPhi \left( t \right)$ 证明:

$$
\varPsi (t) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} n \phi} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} f (t)},
$$

其中

$$
f (t) = \frac {1}{\hbar} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m b ^ {2}} \left(n - \frac {q \varPhi (t ^ {\prime})}{2 \pi \hbar}\right) ^ {2} \mathrm {d} t ^ {\prime}
$$

是合时薛定谤方程的解.

证明 依赖时间的薛定湾方程的一般形式为

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \Psi}{\partial t} = H \Psi .
$$

等式左边可将被函数的具体形式代入，得

$$
\mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\partial \varPsi}{\partial t} = \hbar f ^ {\prime} (t) \varPsi (t) = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m b ^ {2}} \left[ n - \frac {q \varPhi (t)}{2 \pi \hbar} \right] ^ {2} \varPsi .
$$

参照方程 (4.200)，等式右边得

$$
\begin{array}{l} H \varPsi = \frac {1}{2 m} \left\{- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{b ^ {2}} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial \phi^ {2}} + \left[ \frac {q \varPhi (t)}{2 \pi b} \right] ^ {2} + \mathrm {i} \frac {\hbar q \varPhi (t)}{\pi b ^ {2}} \frac {\partial}{\partial \phi} \right\} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} n \phi} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} f (t)} \\ = \frac {1}{2 m} \left\{\frac {\hbar^ {2}}{b ^ {2}} n ^ {2} + \left[ \frac {q \varPhi (t)}{2 \pi b} \right] ^ {2} - \frac {\hbar q \varPhi (t)}{\pi b ^ {2}} n \right\} \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} n \phi} \mathrm {e} ^ {- \mathrm {i} f (t)} \\ = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m b ^ {2}} \left[ n - \frac {q \Phi (t)}{2 \pi \hbar} \right] ^ {2} \Psi . \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，依赖时间的薛定湾方程两边是相等的，即 $\varPsi \left( t \right)$ 是其一个解.

习题 例题 中的能级移动可以从经典电动力学方面进行理解.考虑初始没有电流流进螺线管的情况.想象缓慢增加电流.

通过通量的改变计算(经典电动力学)电磁场，并证明限制在环上的电荷做功的速率可以写为

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} W}{\mathrm {d} \varPhi} = - q \frac {\omega}{2 \pi},
$$

其中 $\omega$ 是粒子的角速度.

(b) 计算机械角动量的 $z$ 分量，①

$$
\boldsymbol {L} _ {\text {机 械}} = \boldsymbol {r} \times m \boldsymbol {v} = \boldsymbol {r} \times (\boldsymbol {p} - q \boldsymbol {A}),
$$

对于例题 4.6 中处在 $\psi _ { n }$ 态的粒子.注意机械角动量不是以元 $\hbar$ 的整数倍量子化的.②

(c) 证明:从 (a) 部分得到的结果精确地等于通量增加时定态能量改变的速率$\mathrm { d } E _ { n } / \mathrm { d } \varPhi$

解答 (时由法拉第定律，通量的改变可以产生电磁场

$$
\oint \boldsymbol {E} \cdot \mathrm {d} \boldsymbol {l} = - \frac {\mathrm {d} \varPhi}{\mathrm {d} t},
$$

$$
E 2 \pi b = - \frac {\mathrm {d} \varPhi}{\mathrm {d} t},
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {E} = - \frac {1}{2 \pi b} \frac {\mathrm {d} \varPhi}{\mathrm {d} t} \hat {\phi}.
$$

电荷沿着线圈移动 $\mathrm { d } l = v \mathrm { d } t$ 所做的功为

$$
\mathrm {d} W = q E \mathrm {d} l = - \frac {q}{2 \pi b} \frac {\mathrm {d} \Phi}{\mathrm {d} t} v \mathrm {d} t = - \frac {q v}{2 \pi b} \mathrm {d} \Phi .
$$

由角速度 $\omega = v / b$ ，得

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} W}{\mathrm {d} \varPhi} = - \frac {q \omega}{2 \pi}.
$$

(b) 机械角动量需要采用坐标与正则动量的叉乘得出，由方程 (4.192)

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} \left\langle \boldsymbol {r} \right\rangle}{\mathrm {d} t} = \frac {1}{m} \left\langle (\boldsymbol {p} - q \boldsymbol {A}) \right\rangle ,
$$

可知正则动量为 $p - q A$ 因此机械角动量为

$$
\pmb {L} _ {\text {机 械}} = \pmb {r} \times (\pmb {p} - q \pmb {A}) = (\pmb {r} \times \pmb {p}) - q (\pmb {r} \times \pmb {A}) = \pmb {L} - q \left(r \frac {\Phi}{2 \pi r}\right) (\hat {r} \times \hat {\phi}) = \pmb {L} - q \frac {\Phi}{2 \pi} \hat {k},
$$

其中 $A = \frac { \varPhi } { 2 \pi r } \hat { \phi }$ ，见方程 (4 8)

困此，机械角动量 $z$ 轴方向的分量为

$$
[ \pmb {L} _ {\text {机 械}} ] _ {z} = L _ {z} - q \frac {\varPhi}{2 \pi} = - \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} \phi} - \frac {q \varPhi}{2 \pi}.
$$

将其作用在例题 4.6 中的 $\psi _ { n } = A \mathrm { e } ^ { \mathrm { i } n \phi }$ 态上，得

$$
[ \pmb {L} _ {\text {机 械}} ] _ {z} \psi_ {n} = \left(- \mathrm {i} \hbar \frac {\mathrm {d}}{\mathrm {d} \phi} - \frac {q \varPhi}{2 \pi}\right) \left(A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} n \phi}\right) = - \mathrm {i} \hbar (\mathrm {i} n) A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} n \phi} - \frac {q \varPhi}{2 \pi} A \mathrm {e} ^ {\mathrm {i} \lambda \phi} = \left(n \hbar - \frac {q \varPhi}{2 \pi}\right) \psi_ {n}.
$$

机械角动量的本征值为 $\left( n \hbar - { \frac { q \phi } { 2 \pi } } \right)$ .机械角动量不是以 $\hbar$ 的整数倍量子化的.

(c) 对本征值 $E _ { n } = \frac { \hbar ^ { 2 } } { 2 m b ^ { 2 } } \left( n - \frac { q \varPhi } { 2 \pi \hbar } \right) ^ { \frac { 2 } { 2 } }$ <Þ 2 (方程 求导，得

$$
\frac {\mathrm {d} E _ {n}}{\mathrm {d} \varPhi} = \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m b ^ {2}} 2 \left(n - \frac {q \varPhi}{2 \pi \hbar}\right) \left(- \frac {q}{2 \pi \hbar}\right) = - \frac {L _ {\text {机 械}}}{m b ^ {2}} \frac {q}{2 \pi} = - \frac {L _ {\text {机 械}}}{I} \frac {q}{2 \pi} = - \omega \frac {q}{2 \pi},
$$

其中 $I = m b ^ { 2 }$ 为转动惯量，机械角动量写为 $L _ { \mathrm { \# I \# \# } } = I \omega$

# 章全同粒子

![](images/9b36129079e0dd0534b98c1600af3733cfec0bc2fe374e58193f1f5dab60b804.jpg)

# 本章主要内容概要

# 1.合阉往事

质量、电荷、自旋等固有性质完全相同的微观粒子称为全同粒子在一个量子体系中全同粒子是不可区分的，两全同拉子相王支换不会引起物理性质的改变(全同性原理).所有的微观拉子可以分为两类:玻色于和费未子.

# 2. Ilt色毒和费*!

所有自旋为昆整数倍的粒子称为玻色子，而所有自旋为 $\hbar / 2$ 奇数倍的粒子称为费米子.由费米子组成的量子体草，不能有两个或两个以上的费米于处于同一个状态(泡利不相容原理)，体系的波函数在交换任意两个费米子时是反对称的.对由玻色子组成的量子体系，则不受泡利不相容原理的限制，两个或两个以上的玻色子可以处于同一个状态，体革的波函数在交换任意两个玻色于时是对称的.

如果体系的波函数可以由归一化的单粒子波函数 $\phi _ { i } \left( q _ { \alpha } \right)$ 的积表示，其中 $i$ 表示不同的单粒子态， $q _ { \alpha }$ 表示第 $\alpha$ 个粒子的量子数(包括空间与自旋)，则由 $N$ 个费米子组成体系的反对称波函数可以用 $N$ 阶行列式表示为

$$
\varPhi_{A}\left(q_{1},q_{2},\dots ,q_{\alpha},\dots ,q_{N}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N!}}\left| \begin{array}{cccc}\phi_{i}\left(q_{1}\right)&\phi_{i}\left(q_{2}\right)&\cdots&\phi_{i}\left(q_{N}\right)\\ \phi_{j}\left(q_{1}\right)&\phi_{j}\left(q_{2}\right)&\cdots&\phi_{j}\left(q_{N}\right)\\ \cdots&\cdots&\cdots&\cdots\\ \phi_{k}\left(q_{1}\right)&\phi_{k}\left(q_{2}\right)&\cdots&\phi_{k}\left(q_{N}\right)\end{array} \right|,
$$

交换任何两个粒子就是交换行列式中的两列，这使行列式改变符号，即波函数 $\varPhi _ { A }$ 在交换两粒子时是反对称的.当任意两粒子处于相同状态，即行列式中两行相同时，行列式为零，表示不能有两个或两个以上的费米子处于同一个状态.

对由 $N$ 个玻色于组成的体系，体系的对称波函数可以表示为

$$
\Phi_ {A} \left(q _ {1}, q _ {2}, \dots , q _ {\alpha}, \dots , q _ {N}\right) = C \sum_ {P} P \phi_ {i} \left(q _ {1}\right) \phi_ {j} \left(q _ {2}\right) \dots \phi_ {k} \left(q _ {N}\right),
$$

其中 $P$ 表示 $N$ 个拉子在就函数中的某一种排列， $\sum _ { P }$ :表示对所有可能排列求和，由于玻色子可以处于相同的状态， $i , j , \cdots , k$ 可以相等， $C$ 是归一化常数 $i , j , \cdots , k$ 完全相等时，全不相等时为 $1 / \sqrt { N ! }$

# 3. :i:恭 JJ

以两拉子体系为例，若体系的波函数可以表示为空间部分和自旋部分之积，对称和

反对称的空间波函数为

$$
\psi_ {\pm} \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {a} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {b} \left(x _ {2}\right) \pm \psi_ {b} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {a} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right].
$$

这种波函数对称化的妥求会使两粒子间出现一种力的作用，称为支换力.在对称空间波函数中，这个力是吸引力，倾向于把两粒子拉近;在反对称空间波函数中，这个力是排斥力，倾向于让两拉子相互远离.罔体中属于不同原子的两个电子组成的共价键可以由这种力解释，两电子体革的玻函数是反对称的，当两个电子的自旋波函数为反对称的自旋羊态时，空间就函数必是对称的，所以这种状态下的两个电子倾向于相互靠近，形成共价键.

# 4. 乱番周翩J.

原子中一个单粒子态 $( n , \ell , m )$ 称为轨道，因为电子是费米子，受到泡利不相容原理的制约，一个轨道上只能有两个电子(一个自旋向上，一个自旋向下).当原子处于基态时，电子将从最低能态开始依据洪德定则依次填充 $n = 1$ 这个壳层能容纳两个电子，$n = 2$ 壳层能容纳 个电子， $n = 3$ 壳层能容纳 18 个电子，第 $n$ 个壳层可以容纳 $2 n ^ { 2 }$ 个电子 (洪德第一定则:在其他量都相同时，总自旋 $( S )$ 取最大值的状态的能量最低.洪德第二定则:当自旋给定时，总轨道角量子数 $( L )$ 取最大值且同整体的反对称性一致时，将具有最低的能量.洪德第二定则:如果次壳层 $( n , \ell )$ 填充不到一半，则能量最低态满足 $J = | L - S |$ 如果填充超过一半，则 $J = L + S$ 态能量最低.)一般以 ${ } ^ { 2 S + 1 } L _ { J }$ 表示原子电子组态，其中 $S$ 为电子总自旋角动量 $L$ 为总轨道角动量 $J$ 为总角动量量子数.

# 周剿恤 'φ 命曲曲.带储精

若势场满足周期性条件 $V \left( x + a \right) = V \left( x \right)$ ，则薛定诗方程的解满足 $\psi \left( x + a \right) =$ $ { \mathrm { e } } ^ { \mathrm { i } K a } \psi \left( x \right)$ (布洛赫定理).在周期性势场中，电子的能组呈能带结构，分为禁带和允带，没有电子的能态可以处在禁带的能量范围.如果电子依次填充允带，最高能带被完全填满，此时若要激发一个电子就需要一个较大的能量，因为电子需要跳过一个禁带，这样的材料称为绝缘体.相反地，如果最高能带走部分填充的，激发一个电子只需要一个很小的能量，这种材料通常为导体.

# 6. 费事纯针

在绝对零度，一个物理系统将处在它的能量最低状态(体系的基态).当温皮升高时，随机的热激发将开始占据激发态，当处于热平衡状态时，微观粒子在能态的分布(相对几率)为

$$
n \left(\varepsilon\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {{\mathrm {e} ^ {- (\varepsilon - \mu) / k _ {\mathrm {B}} T},}} & {{\mathrm {麦 克 斯 韦} - \mathrm {玻 尔 兹 曼 分 布 适 用 于 可 分 辨 粒 子}}} \\ {{\frac {1}{\mathrm {e} ^ {(\varepsilon - \mu) / k _ {\mathrm {B}} T} + 1},}} & {{\mathrm {费 米} - \mathrm {狄 拉 克 分 布 适 用 于 费 米 子}}} \\ {{\frac {1}{\mathrm {e} ^ {(\varepsilon - \mu) / k _ {\mathrm {B}} T} - 1},}} & {{\mathrm {玻 色} - \mathrm {爱 因 斯 坦 分 布 适 用 于 玻 色 子}}} \end{array} \right.
$$

串串习题 5.1 通常情况下，相互作用势的大小仅依赖于两粒子间的相对位移矢量

$r \equiv r _ { 1 } - r _ { 2 } : V ( r _ { 1 } , r _ { 2 } )  V ( r )$ 在该情况下，将变量 $\mathbf { \boldsymbol { r } } _ { 1 } , \mathbf { \boldsymbol { r } } _ { 2 }$ 代换为 $\mathbfit { \Delta } \mathbf { r }$ 和 $\pmb { R } \equiv$ $( m _ { 1 } { \pmb r } _ { 1 } + m _ { 2 } { \pmb r } _ { 2 } ) / ( m _ { 1 } + m _ { 2 } )$ (质心坐标)，薛定谆方程就可以进行分离变量.

(a) 证明: $r _ { 1 } = R + \left( \mu / m _ { 1 } \right) r , r _ { 2 } = R - \left( \mu / m _ { 2 } \right) r$ 和 $\nabla _ { 1 } = ( \mu / m _ { 2 } ) \nabla _ { R } +$ $\nabla _ { r } , \nabla _ { 2 } = \left( \mu / m _ { 1 } \right) \nabla _ { R } - \nabla _ { r }$ ，其中 $\mu \equiv \frac { m _ { 1 } m _ { 2 } } { m _ { 1 } + m _ { 2 } }$ m1m2 是体系的约化质量.  
(b) 证明: (定态)薛定诗方程(方程 (5.η)

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 (m _ {1} + m _ {2})} \nabla_ {R} ^ {2} \psi - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 \mu} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} \psi + V (\boldsymbol {r}) \psi = E \psi .
$$

(c) 分离变量，令 $\psi \left( R , r \right) = \psi _ { R } \left( R \right) \psi _ { r } \left( r \right)$ 注意到 $\psi _ { R }$ 满足总质量 $m$ 为$( m _ { 1 } + m _ { 2 } )$ 势能为萃，能量为 $E _ { R }$ 的单粒子薛定愕方程 $\psi _ { r }$ 满足质量 $m$ 为约化质量，势能为 $V \left( r \right)$ 能量为 $E _ { r }$ 的单粒子薛定潜方程系统的总能量为两者之和:$E = E _ { R } + E _ { r }$ 这告诉我们质心的运动像一个自由粒子的运动，而相对运动(即粒子相对于粒子 的运动)可以看作以约化质量为质量，处于势场 $V$ 中的单粒子的运动.在经典力学中存在完全类似的分解方法ifD 用这种方法可以将两体问题简化为等价的单体问题.

解答 (a) 由相对位置坐标 $r \equiv r _ { 1 } - r _ { 2 }$ 和质心坐标 ${ \pmb R } \equiv ( m _ { 1 } { \pmb r } _ { 1 } + m _ { 2 } { \pmb r } _ { 2 } ) / ( m _ { 1 } + m _ { 2 } )$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \boldsymbol {R} = m _ {1} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} + m _ {2} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} = m _ {1} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} + m _ {2} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1} - \boldsymbol {r}) = (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} - m _ {2} \boldsymbol {r}, \\ \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} = \boldsymbol {R} + \frac {m _ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \boldsymbol {r} = \boldsymbol {R} + \frac {\mu}{m _ {1}} \boldsymbol {r}, \\ (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \boldsymbol {R} = m _ {1} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} + m _ {2} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} = m _ {1} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2} + \boldsymbol {r}) + m _ {2} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} = (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} + m _ {1} \boldsymbol {r}, \\ \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} = \boldsymbol {R} - \frac {m _ {1}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \boldsymbol {r} = \boldsymbol {R} - \frac {\mu}{m _ {2}} \boldsymbol {r}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$\nabla _ { 1 }$ 和 $\nabla _ { 2 }$ 意义为分别对第一个粒子的坐标和第二个粒子的坐标作微分，要证明 $\nabla _ { 1 } = \left( \mu / m _ { 2 } \right) \nabla _ { R } +$ $\nabla _ { { r } }$ 和 $\nabla _ { 2 } = \left( \mu / m _ { 1 } \right) \nabla _ { R } - \nabla _ { r }$ ，很明显是将对单个粒子绝对坐标的微分转化为对两个粒子相对坐标和体系质心坐标的微分.

为方便起见，记 $\pmb { R } = \left( X , Y , Z \right) , \pmb { r } = \left( x , y , z \right)$ 我们先对三个变量中的一个 $( x )$ 进行微分，然后推广到二维，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\nabla_ {1}\right) _ {x} = \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {1}} = \frac {\partial X}{\partial x _ {1}} \frac {\partial}{\partial X} + \frac {\partial x}{\partial x _ {1}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} = \frac {m _ {1}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \frac {\partial}{\partial X} + \frac {\partial}{\partial x} = \frac {\mu}{m _ {2}} \frac {\partial}{\partial X} + \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \\ = \frac {\mu}{m _ {2}} \left(\nabla_ {R}\right) _ {x} + \left(\nabla_ {r}\right) _ {x} \Rightarrow \nabla_ {1} = \frac {\mu}{m _ {2}} \nabla_ {R} + \nabla_ {r}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(\nabla_ {2}\right) _ {x} = \frac {\partial}{\partial x _ {2}} = \frac {\partial X}{\partial x _ {2}} \frac {\partial}{\partial X} + \frac {\partial x}{\partial x _ {2}} \frac {\partial}{\partial x} = \frac {m _ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \frac {\partial}{\partial X} - \frac {\partial}{\partial x} = \frac {\mu}{m _ {1}} \frac {\partial}{\partial X} - \frac {\partial}{\partial x} \\ = \frac {\mu}{m _ {1}} \left(\nabla_ {R}\right) _ {x} - \left(\nabla_ {r}\right) _ {x} \Rightarrow \nabla_ {2} = \frac {\mu}{m _ {1}} \nabla_ {R} - \nabla_ {r}. \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) 将(叫中的结论代入两粒子的哈密顿量中，得

$$
H = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m _ {1}} \nabla_ {1} ^ {2} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m _ {2}} \nabla_ {2} ^ {2} + V (\boldsymbol {r})
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m _ {1}} \left[ \left(\mu / m _ {2}\right) \nabla_ {R} + \nabla_ {r} \right] ^ {2} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m _ {2}} \left[ \left(\mu / m _ {1}\right) \nabla_ {R} - \nabla_ {r} \right] ^ {2} + V (\boldsymbol {r}) \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m _ {1} m _ {2}} \left[ \frac {\mu^ {2}}{m _ {2}} \nabla_ {R} ^ {2} + m _ {2} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} + \mu \left(\nabla_ {R} \nabla_ {r} + \nabla_ {r} \nabla_ {R}\right) + \frac {\mu^ {2}}{m _ {1}} \nabla_ {R} ^ {2} \right. \\ \left. + m _ {1} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} - \mu (\nabla_ {R} \nabla_ {r} + \nabla_ {r} \nabla_ {R}) \right] + V (\boldsymbol {r}) \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m _ {1} m _ {2}} \left(\mu \nabla_ {R} ^ {2} + m _ {1} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} + m _ {2} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2}\right) + V (\boldsymbol {r}) \\ = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 (m _ {1} + m _ {2})} \nabla_ {R} ^ {2} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 \mu} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} + V (\boldsymbol {r}), \\ \end{array}
$$

所以定态薛定诗方程为

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 (m _ {1} + m _ {2})} \nabla_ {R} ^ {2} \psi - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 \mu} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} \psi + V (\boldsymbol {r}) \psi = E \psi .
$$

(c) 采用分离变量法，方程两边同除以 $\psi _ { R } \left( { \pmb R } \right) \psi _ { r } \left( { \pmb r } \right)$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \psi_ {r} (\boldsymbol {r})} \nabla_ {R} ^ {2} \psi_ {R} (\boldsymbol {R}) - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 \mu \psi_ {R} (\boldsymbol {R})} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} \psi_ {r} (\boldsymbol {r}) + V (\boldsymbol {r}) = E \\ \Rightarrow - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \psi_ {r}} \nabla_ {R} ^ {2} \psi_ {R} = E - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 \mu \psi_ {R}} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} \psi_ {r} - V (\boldsymbol {r}). \\ \end{array}
$$

令方程两边同时等于常量 $E _ { R }$ ，则

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \psi_ {r}} \nabla_ {R} ^ {2} \psi_ {R} = E _ {R},
$$

$$
E + \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 \mu \psi_ {R}} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} \psi_ {r} - V (\boldsymbol {r}) = E _ {R}.
$$

上式可以写为

$$
- \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 \mu \psi_ {R}} \nabla_ {r} ^ {2} \psi_ {r} + V (\boldsymbol {r}) = E _ {r}, (E _ {r} \equiv E - E _ {R}).
$$

$\psi _ { R } \left( R \right)$ 满足总质量为 $( m _ { 1 } + m _ { 2 } )$ 势能为零，能量为 $E _ { R }$ 的单粒子薛定诲方程 $\psi _ { r } \left( r \right)$ 满足总质量为 $\mu$ ，势能为 $V \left( r \right)$ 能量为 $E _ { r }$ 的单粒子薛是湾方程，且 $E = E _ { R } + E _ { r }$

习题 5.2 针对习题 5.1，可以简单地用约化质量代替电子质量来修正氢原子核的运动.

求在计算氢原子结合能(方程 使用 $m$ 而不是 $\mu$ 时，产生的误差百分比(精确到两位有效数字).  
(b) 求氢和氛(原子核既含有质子又含有中子)的红色巴耳末线 $\stackrel { \prime } { n } = 3  n =$ 2) 之间的波长差.   
求电子偶素的结合能(氢原子中质子被正电子取代，正电子与电子质量相同，但电荷相反).  
假设你想证实 $\mu$ 介子氧的存在，其中电子被 $\mu$ 介子(电荷相同，但重量是电子的 206.77 倍)取代.在哪里(即在什么波长下)寻找"莱曼- $\cdot \mathrm { { \alpha } } ^ { \prime \mathit { \check { \Psi } } }$ 线 $\ n = 2  n = 1$ )?

解答 (a) 氢原子中电子的约化质量为

$$
\mu = \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}} m _ {\mathrm {p}}}{m _ {\mathrm {e}} + m _ {\mathrm {p}}} = \frac {0 . 5 1 1 \times 9 3 8 . 2 7 2}{0 . 5 1 1 + 9 3 8 . 2 7 2} \mathrm {M e V} = 0. 5 1 0 7 \mathrm {M e V}.
$$

根据方程 (4.77) ，氢原子基态 $( n = 1 )$ ) 能量(结合能)应为

$$
E _ {1} = - \left[ \frac {\mu}{2 \hbar^ {2}} \left(\frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2} \right].
$$

可知 $E _ { 1 }$ 与厨量呈正比关系，所以当用电子质量 $m$ 取代的化质量 $\mu$ 时，产生的误差为

$$
\frac {\Delta E _ {1}}{E _ {1}} = \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}} - \mu}{\mu} = \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}} - m _ {\mathrm {e}} m _ {\mathrm {p}} / \left(m _ {\mathrm {e}} + m _ {\mathrm {p}}\right)}{m _ {\mathrm {e}} m _ {\mathrm {p}} / \left(m _ {\mathrm {e}} + m _ {\mathrm {p}}\right)} = \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}}}{m _ {\mathrm {p}}} = \frac {9.109 \times 10 ^ {- 31} \mathrm {kg}}{1.673 \times 10 ^ {- 31} \mathrm {kg}} = 0.054 \%
$$

(b) 由方程 (4.94)，用约化质量 $\mu$ 表示的重德伯常量为

$$
\mathfrak {R} \equiv \frac {\mu}{4 \pi c \hbar^ {3}} \left(\frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}}\right) ^ {2}.
$$

正比于质量，当电子从 $n = 3$ 向 $n = 2$ 能级跃迁时，辐射的波长为

$$
\lambda = \frac {1}{\Re \left(\frac {1}{2 ^ {2}} - \frac {1}{3 ^ {2}}\right)} = \frac {3 6}{5 \Re}.
$$

对于氢原 $\mu _ { \mathrm { H } } = { \frac { m _ { \mathrm { e } } m _ { \mathrm { p } } } { m _ { \mathrm { e } } + m _ { \mathrm { p } } } }$ memp ，对于负 $\mu _ { \mathrm { { D } } } = { \frac { m _ { \mathrm { { e } } } \left( 2 m _ { \mathrm { { p } } } \right) } { m _ { \mathrm { { e } } } + 2 m _ { \mathrm { { p } } } } }$ ，所以波长差为me -t- mp

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \lambda = \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} - \lambda_ {\mathrm {D}} = \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} \left(1 - \frac {\lambda_ {\mathrm {D}}}{\lambda_ {\mathrm {H}}}\right) = \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} \left(1 - \frac {\Re_ {\mathrm {H}}}{\Re_ {\mathrm {D}}}\right) = \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} \left(1 - \frac {\mu_ {\mathrm {H}}}{\mu_ {\mathrm {D}}}\right) \\ = \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} \left[ 1 - \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}} m _ {\mathrm {p}} \left(m _ {\mathrm {e}} + 2 m _ {\mathrm {p}}\right)}{m _ {\mathrm {e}} \left(2 m _ {\mathrm {p}}\right) \left(m _ {\mathrm {e}} + m _ {\mathrm {p}}\right)} \right] = \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} \left[ 1 - \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}} + 2 m _ {\mathrm {p}}}{2 \left(m _ {\mathrm {e}} + m _ {\mathrm {p}}\right)} \right] \\ = \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}}}{2 (m _ {\mathrm {e}} + m _ {\mathrm {p}})} \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} \approx \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}}}{2 m _ {\mathrm {p}}} \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} = \frac {3 6}{5 \Re} = \frac {3 6}{5 \times 1 . 0 9 7 \times 1 0 ^ {7}} \mathrm {m} = 6. 5 6 3 \times 1 0 ^ {- 7} \mathrm {m},
$$

$$
\Delta \lambda = \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}}}{2 m _ {\mathrm {p}}} \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} = \frac {9 . 1 0 9 \times 1 0 ^ {- 3 1}}{2 \times 1 . 6 7 3 \times 1 0 ^ {- 2 7}} \times 6. 5 6 3 \times 1 0 ^ {- 7} \mathrm {m} = 1. 7 8 7 \times 1 0 ^ {- 1 0} \mathrm {m}.
$$

(c) 对电子偶素，其约化质量为 $\mu _ { + } = { \frac { m _ { \mathrm { e } } m _ { \mathrm { e } } } { m _ { \mathrm { e } } + m _ { \mathrm { e } } } } = { \frac { m _ { \mathrm { e } } } { 2 } }$ meme 所以其基态能量(结合能)为

$$
E _ {+} = \frac {\mu_ {+}}{\mu_ {\mathrm {H}}} E _ {\mathrm {H}} \approx \frac {1}{2} E _ {\mathrm {H}} = - 6. 8 \mathrm {e V}.
$$

(d) 对于 $\mu$ 子替换电子后，约化质量对应的里德伯常量要作相应的改变，则

$$
\lambda_ {\mu} = \frac {1}{\mathfrak {R} _ {\mu} \left(\frac {1}{1} - \frac {1}{2 ^ {2}}\right)} = \frac {4}{3 \mathfrak {R} _ {\mu}} = \frac {4}{3 (\mu_ {\mu} / \mu_ {\mathrm {H}}) \mathfrak {R}} = \frac {\mu_ {\mathrm {H}}}{\mu_ {\mu}} \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mu_ {\mathrm {H}}}{\mu_ {\mu}} = \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}} m _ {\mathrm {p}}}{m _ {\mathrm {e}} + m _ {\mathrm {p}}} \frac {m _ {\mu} + m _ {\mathrm {p}}}{m _ {\mu} m _ {\mathrm {p}}} = \frac {m _ {\mathrm {e}}}{m _ {\mu}} \frac {m _ {\mu} + m _ {\mathrm {p}}}{m _ {\mathrm {e}} + m _ {\mathrm {p}}} \\ = \frac {1}{2 0 6 . 7 7} \frac {2 0 6 . 7 7 \times 9 . 1 0 9 \times 1 0 ^ {- 3 1} + 1 . 6 7 3 \times 1 0 ^ {- 2 7}}{9 . 1 0 9 \times 1 0 ^ {- 3 1} + 1 . 6 7 3 \times 1 0 ^ {- 2 7}} = 5. 3 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {- 3}, \\ \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} = \frac {4}{3 \Re} = \frac {1}{0 . 7 5 \times 1 . 0 9 7 \times 1 0 ^ {7}} \mathrm {m} = 1. 2 1 5 \times 1 0 ^ {- 7} \mathrm {m}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以 $\mu$ 介子氢的"莱曼-α" 线的波长为

$$
\lambda_ {\mu} = \frac {\mu_ {\mathrm {H}}}{\mu_ {\mu}} \lambda_ {\mathrm {H}} = 5. 3 7 8 \times 1 0 ^ {- 3} \times 1. 2 1 5 \times 1 0 ^ {- 7} \mathrm {m} = 6. 5 3 4 \times 1 0 ^ {- 1 0} \mathrm {m}.
$$

习题 5.3 氯原子在自然界有两种同位素: $\mathrm { C l ^ { 3 5 } }$ 和 $\mathrm { C l ^ { 3 7 } }$ 证明: HCl 的振动光谱应包含相距很近的双线结构，其能级分裂由 $\Delta \nu = 7 . 5 1 \times 1 0 ^ { - 4 } \nu$ 给出，其中 $\nu$ 是出射光子的频率.提示 把它想象成一个频率为 $\omega = \sqrt { k / \mu }$ 的谐振子，其中 $\mu$ 为约化质量(方程 (5.15))，两种同位素的 $k$ 可以认为近似相同

证明 HOl 的振动可以看作一个频率为 $\omega = \sqrt { k / \mu }$ 的简谐振子， $\mu$ 为的化质量.能量本征值为

$$
E _ {n} = \left(n + \frac {1}{2}\right) \hbar \omega .
$$

当从 $n _ { i }$ 态跃迁到 $n _ { f }$ 态时，辐射光子能量满足

$$
E _ {\mathrm {p}} = h \nu = \Delta E = (n _ {i} - n _ {f}) \hbar \omega = \Delta n \hbar \omega , (\Delta n \equiv n _ {i} - n _ {f}).
$$

(这里没有考虑跃迁时需满足的选择定则对 $\Delta n$ 的限制，它对我们的结果也没有影响.)由于约化质量不同引起的 $\mathrm { C l ^ { 3 5 } }$ 和 $\mathrm { C l ^ { 3 7 } }$ 频率之差为

$$
\Delta \nu = \nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}} - \nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 7}} = \nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}} \left(1 - \frac {\nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 7}}}{\nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}}}\right) = \nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}} \left(1 - \sqrt {\frac {\mu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}}}{\mu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 7}}}}\right).
$$

$$
\frac {\mu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}}}{\mu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 7}}} = \frac {m _ {\mathrm {H}} (3 5 m _ {\mathrm {H}})}{m _ {\mathrm {H}} + 3 5 m _ {\mathrm {H}}} \cdot \frac {m _ {\mathrm {H}} + 3 7 m _ {\mathrm {H}}}{m _ {\mathrm {H}} (3 7 m _ {\mathrm {H}})} = \frac {3 5 \times 3 8}{3 6 \times 3 7} = 0. 9 9 8 5.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \Delta \nu = \nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}} \left(1 - \sqrt {0 . 9 9 8 5}\right) = \nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}} \left(1 - \sqrt {1 - 0 . 0 0 1 5}\right) \\ \approx \nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}} \left[ 1 - \left(1 - \frac {1}{2} \times 0. 0 0 1 5\right) \right] = 7. 5 \times 1 0 ^ {- 4} \nu_ {\mathrm {H C l} ^ {3 5}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

# 串习题 5.4

(a) :如 $\psi _ { a }$ 仇和 $\psi _ { b }$ 是正交归一的，则方程 (5.17) 中的常数 为多少?  
(b) 如果 $\psi _ { a } = \psi _ { b }$ (巳归一化)，则 $A$ 为多少? (当然，只有玻色子才会发生这种情况.)

解答 (功方程 (5.17) 对称和反对称的披函数为

$$
\psi_ {\pm} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) = A \left[ \psi_ {a} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \psi_ {b} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) \pm \psi_ {b} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \psi_ {a} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) \right].
$$

由归一化条件

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = \int \left| \psi_ {\pm} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) \right| ^ {2} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} \\ = | A | ^ {2} \int [ \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \pm \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) ] ^ {*} [ \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \pm \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) ] d ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} d ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} \\ = | A | ^ {2} \left[ \int | \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \int | \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) | \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} \pm \int \psi_ {b} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \int \psi_ {a} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} \right. \\ \left. \pm \int \psi_ {a} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \int \psi_ {b} ^ {*} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} + \int | \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \int | \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) | \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} \right] \\ = | A | ^ {2} (1 \cdot 1 + 0 \cdot 0 + 0 \cdot 0 + 1 \cdot 1) = 2 | A | ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

得 $A = { \frac { 1 } { \sqrt { 2 } } }$

(b)当 $\psi _ { a } = \psi _ { b }$ 时，只有对称的波函数

$$
\psi_ {+} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) = 2 A \psi_ {a} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \psi_ {b} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right).
$$

归一化

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1 = \int | \psi_ {+} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} \\ = | A | ^ {2} \int [ 2 \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) ] ^ {*} [ 2 \psi_ {a} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}) \psi_ {b} (\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) ] d ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} d ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} \\ = 4 \left| A \right| ^ {2} \int \left| \psi_ {a} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}\right) \right| ^ {2} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} \int \left| \psi_ {b} \left(\boldsymbol {r} _ {2}\right) \right| ^ {2} \mathrm {d} ^ {3} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} = 4 \left| A \right| ^ {2}, \\ A = \frac {1}{2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

# 习题 5.5

(a) 写出处于无限深方势阱中两个无相互作用的全同粒子的哈密顿量，证明:例题 5.1 给出的费米子基态是 $\hat { H }$ 本征函数，且具有适当的本征值，  
求接下来的两个激发态(除了例题中给出的)在这三种情况(可分辨，全同玻色子，全同费米子)下的波函数、能量和简并度.

解答 (a) 哈密顿量为

$$
H = - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {2}} + V (x _ {1}, x _ {2}),
$$

其中

$$
V \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 0 & \left(0 \leqslant x _ {1}, x _ {2} \leqslant a\right) \\ \infty & \left(\text {其 他}\right) \end{array} \right..
$$

定态薛定湾方程为

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2} \psi}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {2}} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \frac {\partial^ {2} \psi}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {2}} = E \psi & (0 \leqslant x _ {1}, x _ {2} \leqslant a) \\ \psi = 0 & (\text {其 他}) \end{array} \right.
$$

例题 给出的费米子基态的披函数是(注意这里没有考虑自旋，你可认为这两个全同粒子处于相同的自旋态)

$$
\psi = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{a} \bigg [ \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) - \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) \bigg ],
$$

具有反对称性，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\partial^ {2} \psi}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {2}} = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{a} \left[ - \left(\frac {\pi}{a}\right) ^ {2} \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) + \left(\frac {2 \pi}{a}\right) ^ {2} \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) \right], \\ \frac {\partial^ {2} \psi}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {2}} = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{a} \left[ - \left(\frac {2 \pi}{a}\right) ^ {2} \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) + \left(\frac {\pi}{a}\right) ^ {2} \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {\hbar^ {2}}{2 m} \left(\frac {\partial^ {2} \psi}{\partial x _ {1} ^ {2}} + \frac {\partial^ {2} \psi}{\partial x _ {2} ^ {2}}\right) = \frac {5 \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} \psi = E \psi \\ \Rightarrow \text {本 征 值} E = \frac {5 \pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}} = 5 K \quad \left(K \equiv \frac {\pi^ {2} \hbar^ {2}}{2 m a ^ {2}}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

(b) 可分辨粒子:

$$
E = 8 K, \quad \psi_ {2 2} = \frac {2}{a} \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right),
$$

$$
E = 1 0 K, \quad \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {\psi_ {1 3} =  \frac {2}{a} \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {3 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right)} \\ {\psi_ {3 1} =  \frac {2}{a} \sin \left(\frac {3 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {2}}{a}\right)} \end{array} \right. \quad . \quad \text {二 重 简 并}
$$

全同玻色子:

$$
E = 8 K, \quad \psi_ {2 2} = \frac {2}{a} \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right),
$$

$$
E = 1 0 K, \quad \psi_ {1 3} = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{a} \left[ \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {3 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) + \sin \left(\frac {3 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) \right].
$$

全同费米子:

$$
E = 1 0 K, \quad \psi_ {1 3} = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{a} \left[ \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {3 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) - \sin \left(\frac {3 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {\pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) \right],
$$

$$
E = 1 3 K, \quad \psi_ {2 3} = \frac {\sqrt {2}}{a} \left[ \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {3 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) - \sin \left(\frac {3 \pi x _ {1}}{a}\right) \sin \left(\frac {2 \pi x _ {2}}{a}\right) \right].
$$

*习题 想象处于无限深方势阱中质量均为 $m$ 的两个无相互作用的粒子，如果一个粒子处于 $\psi _ { n }$ 态(方程 (2.28)) ，另一个粒子处于 $\psi _ { l } \left( l \neq n \right)$ 态，计算 $\left. \left( x _ { 1 } - x _ { 2 } \right) ^ { 2 } \right.$ .假定: 粒子是可分辨的， 粒子为全同玻色子， 粒子为全同费米子

解答设无限探方势阱为

$$
V \left(x\right) = {\left\{ \begin{array}{l l} {0} & {(0 \leqslant x \leqslant a)} \\ {\infty} & {(\text {其 他})} \end{array} \right.} .
$$

则第一个粒子的波函 $\psi _ { n } = { \sqrt { \frac { 2 } { a } } } \sin \left( { \frac { n \pi } { a } } x \right)$ ，第二个粒子的披 $\psi _ { l } = { \sqrt { \frac { 2 } { a } } } \sin \left( { \frac { l \pi } { a } } x \right)$ 叫于)

(叫粒子可分辨时，两粒子波函数为

$$
\psi = \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \left(\frac {n \pi}{a} x _ {1}\right) \sqrt {\frac {2}{a}} \sin \left(\frac {l \pi}{a} x _ {2}\right),
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle = \left\langle x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 x _ {1} x _ {2} \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \mid x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 x _ {1} x _ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} - 2 \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {1} x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ - 2 \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle x _ {1} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} + \left\langle x _ {2} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l} - 2 \left\langle x _ {1} \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x _ {2} \right\rangle_ {l} \\ = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {l}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} = \frac {2}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi}{a} x\right) \mathrm {d} x = \frac {2}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x ^ {2} \frac {1 - \cos (2 n \pi x / a)}{2} \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x ^ {2} \mathrm {d} x - \frac {1}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x ^ {2} \cos \left(\frac {2 n \pi}{a} x\right) \mathrm {d} x = \frac {a ^ {2}}{3} - \frac {a ^ {2}}{2 \left(n \pi\right) ^ {2}}. \quad (\text {见 习 题} 2. 4) \\ \end{array}
$$

因此

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left<   x ^ {2} \right> _ {l} = a ^ {2} \left[ \frac {1}{3} - \frac {1}{2 (l \pi) ^ {2}} \right], \\ \langle x \rangle_ {n} = \frac {2}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \sin^ {2} \left(\frac {n \pi}{a} x\right) \mathrm {d} x = \frac {1}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \left[ 1 - \cos \left(\frac {2 n \pi}{a} x\right) \right] \mathrm {d} x = \frac {a}{2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

同理 $\langle x \rangle _ { l } = \frac { a } { 2 }$ 所以

$$
\left\langle (x _ {1} - x _ {2}) ^ {2} \right\rangle = a ^ {2} \left[ \frac {1}{6} - \frac {1}{2 \pi^ {2}} \left(\frac {1}{n ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{l ^ {2}}\right) \right].
$$

(b时)全同玻色子时，波函数耍满足交换对称性关系

$$
\psi = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {n} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {2}) + \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {1}) \right].
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle \\ = \left\langle \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 x _ {1} x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) + \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 x _ {1} x _ {2}\right) \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) + \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) + \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \mid x _ {1} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) + \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle + \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right. \\ + \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {1}) | x _ {2} ^ {2} | \psi_ {n} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {2}) + \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {1}) \rangle - 2 \times \frac {1}{2} \langle \psi_ {n} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {2}) \\ + \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {1}) | x _ {1} x _ {2} | \psi_ {n} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {2}) + \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {1}) \rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left(\left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle\right) + \frac {1}{2} \left(\left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \right. \\ \left. + \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \mid x _ {2} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle\right) - \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \mid x _ {1} x _ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ - \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {1} x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle - \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ - \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \mid x _ {1} x _ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle , \\ \end{array}
$$

上式前两个括号内的项进行组合，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{2} \left( \right.\left\langle \right. \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right\rangle g + \left\langle \right. \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right)\left. \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right)\left. \right\rangle\left. \right) \\ + \frac {1}{2} \left( \right.\left\langle \right. \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right\rangle + \left\langle \right. \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right)\left. \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right)\left. \right\rangle\left. \right) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {1}{2} \left(\left\langle x _ {1} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} + \left\langle x _ {2} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n}\right) + \frac {1}{2} \left(\left\langle x _ {1} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l} + \left\langle x _ {2} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l}\right) \\ = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l}. \\ \end{array}
$$

后四项结果展开为

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle - \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ - \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle - \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ = - \left\langle x _ {1} \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x _ {2} \right\rangle_ {l} - \left\langle x _ {1} \right\rangle_ {n l} \left\langle x _ {2} \right\rangle_ {l n} - \left\langle x _ {l} \right\rangle_ {l n} \left\langle x _ {2} \right\rangle_ {n l} - \left\langle x _ {1} \right\rangle_ {l} \left\langle x _ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} \\ = - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {l} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n l} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {l n} \\ = - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {l} - 2 \left| \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n l} \right| ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，

$$
\left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {l} - 2 | \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n l} | ^ {2},
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle_ {n l} = \frac {2}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \sin \left(\frac {n \pi}{a} x\right) \sin \left(\frac {l \pi}{a} x\right) \mathrm {d} x \\ = \frac {1}{a} \int_ {0} ^ {a} x \left\{\cos \left[ \frac {(n - l) \pi}{a} x \right] - \cos \left[ \frac {(n + l) \pi}{a} x \right] \right\} d x \\ = \frac {1}{a} \left\{\left[ \frac {a}{(n - l) \pi} \right] ^ {2} \cos \left[ \frac {(n - l) \pi}{a} x \right] + \frac {a x}{(n - l) \pi} x \sin \left[ \frac {(n - l) \pi}{a} x \right] \right. \\ \left. - \left[ \frac {a}{(n + l) \pi} \right] ^ {2} \cos \left[ \frac {(n + l) \pi}{a} x \right] - \frac {a x}{(n + l) \pi} x \sin \left[ \frac {(n + l) \pi}{a} x \right] \right\} \Bigg | _ {0} ^ {a} \\ = \frac {1}{a} \left\{\left[ \frac {a}{(n - l) \pi} \right] ^ {2} (\cos [ (n - l) \pi ] - 1) - \left[ \frac {a}{(n + l) \pi} \right] ^ {2} (\cos [ (n + l) \pi ] - 1) \right\}. \\ \end{array}
$$

但是 $\cos \left[ \left( n \pm l \right) \pi \right] = ( - 1 ) ^ { n \pm l }$ 飞，故

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle_ {n l} = \frac {a}{\pi^ {2}} \left[ (- 1) ^ {n + l} - 1 \right] \left[ \frac {1}{(n - l) ^ {2}} - \frac {1}{(n + l) ^ {2}} \right] \\ = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \frac {- 8 n l a}{\pi^ {2} \left(n ^ {2} - l ^ {2}\right) ^ {2}} & (\text {假 如} n \text {和} l \text {有 相 反 的 奇 偶 性}) \\ 0 & (\text {假 如} n \text {和} l \text {有 相 同 的 奇 偶 性}) \end{array} \right.. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle = a ^ {2} \left[ \frac {1}{6} - \frac {1}{2 \pi^ {2}} \left(\frac {1}{n ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{l ^ {2}}\right) \right] - \frac {1 2 8 n ^ {2} l ^ {2} a ^ {2}}{\pi^ {4} \left(n ^ {2} - l ^ {2}\right) ^ {4}}.
$$

(最后一项具有当 $n$ 和 $l$ 具有相反的奇偶性时才存在.)

全同费米子时 被函数要满足交换反对称性关系

$$
\psi = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right],
$$

则

$$
\left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \left\langle \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 x _ {1} x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 x _ {1} x _ {2}\right) \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \\ + \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \\ - 2 \times \frac {1}{2} \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \mid x _ {1} x _ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left( \right.\left\langle \right. \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right\rangle + \left\langle \right. \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right\rangle\left. \right) + \frac {1}{2} \left( \right.\left\langle \right. \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right)\left. \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right)\left. \right\rangle \\ \left. + \left\langle \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} (x _ {2}) \right\rangle\right) - \left\langle \psi_ {n} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {2}) \right| x _ {1} x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} (x _ {1}) \psi_ {l} (x _ {2}) \right\rangle \\ + \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {1} x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ \left. - \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} x _ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle . \\ \end{array}
$$

上式前两个括号内的项进行组合，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{2} \left(\left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle\right) \\ + \frac {1}{2} \left( \right.\left\langle \right. \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right| x _ {1} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right)\left. \right\rangle + \left\langle \right. \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right)\left. \right| x _ {2} ^ {2} \mid \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right)\left. \right\rangle\left. \right) \\ = \frac {1}{2} \left(\left\langle x _ {1} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} + \left\langle x _ {2} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n}\right) + \frac {1}{2} \left(\left\langle x _ {1} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l} + \left\langle x _ {2} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l}\right) \\ = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l}. \\ \end{array}
$$

后四项结果展开为

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \mid x _ {1} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \mid x _ {2} \mid \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle + \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \mid x _ {1} \mid \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \mid x _ {2} \mid \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ + \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle - \left\langle \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right| x _ {1} \left| \psi_ {l} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right\rangle \left\langle \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right| x _ {2} \left| \psi_ {n} \left(x _ {2}\right) \right\rangle \\ = - \left\langle x _ {1} \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x _ {2} \right\rangle_ {l} + \left\langle x _ {1} \right\rangle_ {n l} \left\langle x _ {2} \right\rangle_ {l n} + \left\langle x _ {l} \right\rangle_ {l n} \left\langle x _ {2} \right\rangle_ {n l} - \left\langle x _ {1} \right\rangle_ {l} \left\langle x _ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} \\ = - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {l} + 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n l} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {l n} \\ = - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {l} + 2 \left| \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n l} \right| ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，

$$
\left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {n} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {l} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {l} + 2 | \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {n l} | ^ {2}.
$$

结合 (b) 的计算结果，可得

$$
\left\langle (x _ {1} - x _ {2}) ^ {2} \right\rangle = a ^ {2} \left[ \frac {1}{6} - \frac {1}{2 \pi^ {2}} \left(\frac {1}{n ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{l ^ {2}}\right) \right] + \frac {1 2 8 n ^ {2} l ^ {2} a ^ {2}}{\pi^ {4} (n ^ {2} - l ^ {2}) ^ {4}}.
$$

(同样，最后一项只有当 $n$ 和 $l$ 具有相反的奇偶性时才存在.)

事* 巳〈主导 习题 5.7 两个无相互作用的粒子(质 相等)处于同一谐振子势中，一个处于基态，另一个处于第一激发态.

(a) 构建波函数 $\psi \left( x _ { 1 } , x _ { 2 } \right)$ (i) 它们是可分辨的， (ii) 它们是全同玻色子，(iii) 它们是全同费米子.分别画出每种情况下的 $| \psi \left( x _ { 1 } , x _ { 2 } \right) | ^ { 2 }$ 采用 Mathematica件中的 Plot3D 指令).

(b) 利用方程 (5.23) (5.25) ，求每种情况下的( $\left. \left( x _ { 1 } - x _ { 2 } \right) ^ { 2 } \right.$   
(c) 利用相对坐标 $r \equiv x _ { 1 } - x _ { 2 }$ 和质心坐标 $R \equiv ( x _ { 1 } + x _ { 2 } ) / 2$ 求每种情况下的$\psi \left( x _ { 1 } , x _ { 2 } \right)$ ' 并对 $R$ 的全部范围进行积分，求两粒子之间距离为什 $| r |$ 时的几率:

$$
P (| r |) = 2 \int | \psi (R, r) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} R
$$

(乘以 是因为 $r$ 可以是正值也可是负值 画出三种情况下的 $P \left( r \right)$ 图.

(d) 定义密度算符

$$
n (x) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \delta (x - x _ {i});
$$

$\langle n \left( x \right) \rangle \mathrm { d } x$ 是处于 $\mathrm { d } x$ 区间内粒子数目的期望值 计算三种情况下的 $\langle n \left( x \right) \rangle$ 并画出你的结果. (结果可能会令你惊讶 )

解答 (a) 构建波函数，首先谐振 的单粒子基态和第一激发态波函数分别为

$$
\psi_ {0} \left(x\right) = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x ^ {2}\right) \text {和} \psi_ {1} \left(x\right) = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} x \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x ^ {2}\right),
$$

对于可分辨粒

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) = \psi_ {0} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {1} \left(x _ {2}\right) \\ = \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {1} ^ {2}\right) \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} x _ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \\ = \left(\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} x _ {2} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x _ {2} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

作图(图 $| \psi \left( x _ { 1 } , x _ { 2 } \right) | ^ { 2 } = \frac { 2 } { \pi } \left( \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } \right) ^ { 2 } x _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } \exp \left[ - \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } \left( x _ { 1 } ^ { 2 } + x _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } \right) \right]$ （万 X~ exp [一于 )因只看形状，为方便起见，令${ \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } } = 1 .$

$$
\begin{array}{l} f [ z _ {-} ] = 2 / \operatorname {P i} * x ^ {\wedge} 2 * \operatorname {E x p} \left[ - (x ^ {\wedge} 2 + y ^ {\wedge} 2) \right]; \\ \begin{array}{l}\text {P l o t 3 D [ f [ z ] , \{x , - 2 . 5 , 2 . 5 \} , \{y , - 2 . 5 , 2 . 5 \} , M e s h \rightarrow 1 0 , P l o t P o i n t s \rightarrow}\\2 0 0 ]\end{array} \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/33f46b63aca906fc0c806176a4e2e203cc4af1c621b708f7c36013a030dc7b42.jpg)  
图5.1

(ii) 对于全同玻色子，波函数要满足交换对称性关系.

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {0} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {1} \left(x _ {2}\right) + \psi_ {0} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {1} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right] \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {1} ^ {2}\right) \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} x _ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \\ + \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} x _ {1} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {1} ^ {2}\right) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} x _ {2} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] \\ + \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} x _ {1} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x _ {2} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] + \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x _ {1} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {1}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \left(x _ {1} + x _ {2}\right) \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

作图(图 5.2): $\left| \psi \left( x _ { 1 } , x _ { 2 } \right) \right| ^ { 2 } = { \frac { 1 } { \pi } } \left( { \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } } \right) ^ { 2 } ( x _ { 1 } + x _ { 2 } ) ^ { 2 } \exp \left[ - { \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } } \left( x _ { 1 } ^ { 2 } + x _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } \right) \right]$ 1 ，同样 ${ \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } } = 1$

$$
f [ z _ {-} ] = 1 / P i * (x + y) ^ {\wedge} 2 * E x p \left[ - (x ^ {\wedge} 2 + y ^ {\wedge} 2) \right];
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l}\text {P l o t 3 D [ f [ z ] , \{x , - 2 . 5 , 2 . 5 \} , \{y , - 2 . 5 , 2 . 5 \} , M e s h \rightarrow 1 0 , P l o t P o i n t s \rightarrow}\\2 0 0 ]\end{array}
$$

![](images/96e47856da48e53bfd591d6458374248ce4c270731f673d2b6e036ae3d68e672.jpg)  
5.2

对于全同费米子 波函数要满足交换反对称关系.

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left[ \psi_ {0} \left(x _ {1}\right) \psi_ {1} \left(x _ {2}\right) - \psi_ {0} \left(x _ {2}\right) \psi_ {1} \left(x _ {1}\right) \right] \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {1} ^ {2}\right) \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} x _ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \\ - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 4} \sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} x _ {1} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} x _ {1} ^ {2}\right) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} x _ {2} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] \\ - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \left(\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} x _ {1} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x _ {2} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] - \frac {1}{\sqrt {2}} \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x _ {1} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] \\ = \sqrt {\frac {1}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

作图(图 $\left| \psi \left( x _ { 1 } , x _ { 2 } \right) \right| ^ { 2 } = { \frac { 1 } { \pi } } \left( { \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } } \right) ^ { 2 } \left( x _ { 1 } - x _ { 2 } \right) ^ { 2 } \exp \left[ - { \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } } \left( x _ { 1 } ^ { 2 } + x _ { 2 } ^ { 2 } \right) \right]$ ，同样 $\frac { m \omega } { \hbar } = 1$

$$
\begin{array}{l} f [ z _ {-} ] = 1 / P i * (x - y) ^ {\wedge} 2 * E x p \left[ - (x ^ {\wedge} 2 + y ^ {\wedge} 2) \right]; \\ \begin{array}{l}\text {P l o t 3 D [ f [ z ] , \{x , - 2 . 5 , 2 . 5 \} , \{y , - 2 . 5 , 2 . 5 \} , M e s h \rightarrow 1 0 , P l o t P o i n t s \rightarrow}\\2 0 0 ]\end{array} \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/944742ec57d91700941829697f01039013d79b3fe6d942e29d4ffef538ea2be8.jpg)  
5.3

(b) 方程 (5.23) 为可分辨粒子的情况

$$
\left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {d} = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {a} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {b} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {a} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {b} = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {0} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {1} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {0} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {1}.
$$

方程 (5.25) 为可分辨粒子的情况，左边取正号为玻色子，取负号为费米子

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {\pm} = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {a} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {b} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {a} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {b} \mp 2 | \langle x \rangle_ {a b} | ^ {2} \\ = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {0} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {1} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {0} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {1} \mp 2 | \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {0 1} | ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由升降阶算符

$$
a = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}} \left(x + \frac {\mathrm {i} p}{m \omega}\right), \quad a ^ {+} = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar}} \left(x - \frac {\mathrm {i} p}{m \omega}\right),
$$

得

$$
x = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} (a ^ {+} + a), \quad p = \mathrm {i} \sqrt {\frac {m \hbar \omega}{2}} (a ^ {+} - a).
$$

利用升降阶算符的性质和本征波函数的正交归 性，计算

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle_ {0} = \langle 0 | \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(a ^ {+} + a\right) | 0 \rangle \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\langle 0 | a ^ {+} | 0 \rangle + \langle 0 | a | 0 \rangle\right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\langle 0 | \sqrt {1} | 1 \rangle + \langle 0 | 0 | 0 \rangle\right) \\ = 0, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle_ {1} = \langle 1 | \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(a ^ {+} + a\right) | 1 \rangle \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\langle 1 | a ^ {+} | 1 \rangle + \langle 1 | a | 1 \rangle\right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\langle 1 | \sqrt {1 + 1} | 2 \rangle + \langle 1 | \sqrt {1} | 0 \rangle\right) \\ = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {0} = \left\langle 0 \right| \left[ \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} (a ^ {+} + a) \right] ^ {2} | 0 \rangle \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\langle 0 | a ^ {+} a ^ {+} | 0 \rangle + \langle 0 | a ^ {+} a | 0 \rangle + \langle 0 | a a ^ {+} | 0 \rangle + \langle 0 | a a | 0 \rangle\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\langle 0 | a ^ {+} \sqrt {1} | 1 \rangle + 0 + \langle 0 | a \sqrt {1} | 1 \rangle + 0\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\langle 0 | \sqrt {1} \sqrt {2} | 2 \rangle + \langle 0 | \sqrt {1} \sqrt {1} | 0 \rangle\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {1} = \left\langle 1 \right| \left[ \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} (a ^ {+} + a) \right] ^ {2} | 1 \rangle \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\langle 1 | a ^ {+} a ^ {+} | 1 \rangle + \langle 1 | a ^ {+} a | 1 \rangle + \langle 1 | a a ^ {+} | 1 \rangle + \langle 1 | a a | 1 \rangle\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\langle 1 | a ^ {+} \sqrt {2} | 2 \rangle + \langle 1 | a ^ {+} \sqrt {1} | 0 \rangle + \langle 1 | a \sqrt {2} | 2 \rangle + \langle 1 | a \sqrt {1} | 0 \rangle\right) \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} \left(\langle 1 | \sqrt {2} \sqrt {3} | 3 \rangle + \langle 1 | \sqrt {1} \sqrt {1} | 1 \rangle + \langle 1 | \sqrt {2} \sqrt {2} | 1 \rangle + \langle 1 | \sqrt {0} \sqrt {1} | 0 \rangle\right) \\ = \frac {3 \hbar}{2 m \omega}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \langle x \rangle_ {0 1} = \langle 0 | \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(a ^ {+} + a\right) | 1 \rangle \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\langle 0 | a ^ {+} | 1 \rangle + \langle 0 | a | 1 \rangle\right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \left(\langle 0 | \sqrt {2} | 2 \rangle + \langle 0 | \sqrt {1} | 0 \rangle\right) \\ = \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

所以，对于可分辨粒子

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {d} = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {0} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {1} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {0} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {1} \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} + \frac {3 \hbar}{2 m \omega} - 0 \\ = \frac {2 \hbar}{m \omega}. \\ \end{array}
$$

对于不可分辨的玻色子和费米子

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\langle \left(x _ {1} - x _ {2}\right) ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {\pm} = \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {0} + \left\langle x ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {1} - 2 \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {0} \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {1} \mp | \left\langle x \right\rangle_ {0 1} | ^ {2} \\ = \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega} + \frac {3 \hbar}{2 m \omega} - 0 \mp 2 \left| \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \right| ^ {2} \\ = \frac {(4 \mp 2) \hbar}{2 m \omega}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此，对于玻色子为 $\frac { \hbar } { m \omega }$ ，对于费米子为主 $\frac { 3 \hbar } { m \omega }$ mw mw

(c) 披函数 $\psi \left( x _ { 1 } , x _ { 2 } \right)$ 用相对坐标 $r = x _ { 1 } - x _ { 2 }$ 和质心坐标 $R = ( x _ { 1 } + x _ { 2 } ) / 2$ 表示为 $\psi \left( r , R \right)$

$$
x _ {1} = R + \frac {r}{2}, \quad x _ {2} = R - \frac {r}{2}, \quad x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2} = \left(R + \frac {r}{2}\right) ^ {2} + \left(R - \frac {r}{2}\right) ^ {2} = 2 R ^ {2} + \frac {r ^ {2}}{2}.
$$

可分辨粒子:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} x _ {2} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] \\ \Rightarrow \psi (R, r) = \sqrt {\frac {2}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \left(R - \frac {r}{2}\right) \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(2 R ^ {2} + \frac {r ^ {2}}{2}\right) \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} P (| r |) = 2 \int | \psi (R, r) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} R \\ = \frac {4}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} r ^ {2}\right) \int_ {- \infty} ^ {+ \infty} \left(R ^ {2} + R r + \frac {r ^ {2}}{4}\right) \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d R \\ = \frac {4}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} r ^ {2}\right) \left[ \int_ {- \infty} ^ {+ \infty} R ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d R \right. \\ + r \int_ {- \infty} ^ {+ \infty} R \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d R + \frac {r ^ {2}}{4} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {+ \infty} \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d R ]. \\ \end{array}
$$

利用函数的奇偶性，中括号内的第一项等于

$$
\begin{array}{l} 2 \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} R ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d R \\ = \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} R \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d R ^ {2} \\ = \left(- \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} R \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) \\ = \left(- \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} R \mathrm {d} \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) \\ = \left(- \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) \left[ R \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) \Big | _ {0} ^ {+ \infty} - \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d R \right] \\ = \left(- \frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) \left[ 0 - \sqrt {\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}} \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {d} \left(\sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} R\right) \right] \\ = \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} \exp (- x ^ {2}) d x \\ = \frac {\sqrt {\pi}}{2} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {3 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $x = \sqrt { \frac { 2 m \omega } { \hbar } } R .$

上式高斯积分部分为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} \exp (- x ^ {2}) d x = \left[ \frac {1}{4} \iint e ^ {- (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2})} d x d y \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ = \left[ \frac {1}{4} \iint \mathrm {e} ^ {- r ^ {2}} r \mathrm {d} r \mathrm {d} \theta \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ = \left[ - \frac {1}{8} \iint \mathrm {e} ^ {- r ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} \left(- r ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {d} \theta \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ = \left[ - \frac {1}{8} \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} \mathrm {e} ^ {- r ^ {2}} \mathrm {d} (- r ^ {2}) \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \mathrm {d} \theta \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ = \left[ - \frac {1}{8} \times 2 \pi \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} \mathrm {d} \left(\mathrm {e} ^ {- r ^ {2}}\right) \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ = \left[ - \frac {1}{8} \times 2 \pi (- 1) \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ = \frac {\sqrt {\pi}}{2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

中括号内第二项 $r \int _ { - \infty } ^ { + \infty } R \exp \left( - \frac { 2 m \omega } { \hbar } R ^ { 2 } \right) \mathrm { d } R$ 2mw 中被积函数为奇函数，积分区间对称，所以为 中括号内第三项可化筒为高斯积分

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {r ^ {2}}{4} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {+ \infty} \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d R \\ = \frac {r ^ {2}}{4} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {1 / 2} 2 \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {d} \left(\sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} R\right) \\ = \frac {r ^ {2}}{2} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} \exp \left(- x ^ {2}\right) \mathrm {d} x, \quad \text {其 中} x = \sqrt {\frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar}} R \\ = \frac {r ^ {2}}{2} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \frac {\sqrt {\pi}}{2} = \frac {\sqrt {\pi} r ^ {2}}{4} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {1 / 2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} P \left(| r |\right) = \frac {4}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} r ^ {2}\right) \left[ \frac {\sqrt {\pi}}{2} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {3 / 2} + 0 + \frac {\sqrt {\pi} r ^ {2}}{4} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \right] \\ = \frac {4}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \frac {\sqrt {\pi}}{4} \left(\frac {\hbar}{m \omega} + r ^ {2}\right) \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} r ^ {2}\right) \\ = \frac {1}{\sqrt {2 \pi}} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \left(\frac {\hbar}{m \omega} + r ^ {2}\right) \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} r ^ {2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

为方便起见， ${ \frac { m \omega } { \hbar } } = 1$ 啊，作图见图

$$
\operatorname {P l o t} [ 1 / (2 \mathrm {P i}) ^ {\wedge} (1 / 2) (\mathrm {x} ^ {\wedge} 2 + 1) \operatorname {E x p} [ - \mathrm {x} ^ {\wedge} 2 / 2 ], \{\mathrm {x}, 0, 5 \} ]
$$

![](images/7134f50731de50ec3c059bfd3a4546486178476439d6d573aef276601838c288.jpg)  
.4

全同玻色子:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \psi \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) = \sqrt {\frac {1}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \left(x _ {1} + x _ {2}\right) \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(x _ {1} ^ {2} + x _ {2} ^ {2}\right) \right] \\ \Rightarrow \psi (R, r) = 2 \sqrt {\frac {1}{\pi}} \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} R \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{2 \hbar} \left(2 R ^ {2} + \frac {r ^ {2}}{2}\right) \right]. \\ P (| r |) = 2 \int | \psi (R, r) | ^ {2} \mathrm {d} R \\ = \frac {8}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \int_ {- \infty} ^ {+ \infty} R ^ {2} \exp \left[ - \frac {m \omega}{\hbar} \left(2 R ^ {2} + \frac {r ^ {2}}{2}\right) \right] d R \\ = \frac {8}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega r ^ {2}}{2 \hbar}\right) \left[ 2 \int_ {0} ^ {+ \infty} R ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {2 m \omega}{\hbar} R ^ {2}\right) d R \right] \\ = \frac {8}{\pi} \left(\frac {m \omega}{\hbar}\right) ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega r ^ {2}}{2 \hbar}\right) \frac {\sqrt {\pi}}{2} \left(\frac {\hbar}{2 m \omega}\right) ^ {3 / 2} \\ = \left(\frac {2 m \omega}{\pi \hbar}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \exp \left(- \frac {m \omega r ^ {2}}{2 \hbar}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$
